{"forms":[{"form":"determiners","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"In quality management, the determiners of quality sometimes differ across use cases.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Someone or something that determines, or helps to determine, something else.","A factor that defines something, or one among several that define it."],"synonyms":[{"word":"definer"}]},{"examples":[{"text":"He is the determiner [usually referring to God (in theological contexts) and job roles (in administrative contexts)]","type":"example"},{"text":"Who is the determiner? [administratively or theologically]","type":"example"},{"text":"The software is the determiner if no input is given before the prompt times out.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Someone or something that determines, or helps to determine, something else.","Someone or something (especially a sentient agent) that determines; a decider of a disposition."],"synonyms":[{"word":"decider"},{"word":"decisor"},{"word":"disposer"},{"word":"definer"},{"word":"arbiter"},{"word":"arbitrator"},{"word":"decisionmaker"}]},{"examples":[{"text":"Definite articles, indefinite articles, and cardinal numbers acting as quantifiers are types of determiners.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A member of a class of words functioning in a noun phrase to identify or distinguish a referent without describing or modifying it."],"synonyms":[{"word":"determinative[Appendix:Glossary"}],"topics":["grammar","human-sciences","linguistics","sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"In the sentence \"Many people voted for him,\" the word many is a determiner.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A dependent function in a noun phrase that marks it as definite or indefinite. This function is usually filled by words in the determinative class but may be filled by other elements such as a genitive pronoun."],"synonyms":[{"word":"determinative[Appendix:Glossary"}],"topics":["grammar","human-sciences","linguistics","sciences"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/dɪˈtɜː.mɪ.nə(ɹ)/","tags":["Australia","Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/dɪˈtɝ.mɪ.nɚ/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":186702,"upstream_offset":896064983,"upstream_record_sha256":"313685cbcdab1345999acf54a30f8d7660a7015bc325df18c850631c9d42803d","word":"determiner"}
